# Blue Archive story inventory

Snapshot 2026-10-20. 1,659 readable stories in 110 collections. This list also retains 0 unavailable entries. [All games](../content-inventory.md).

Original Japanese dialogue clips in 10 story segments. English text uses those same Japanese clips; there is no English dub. Most story segments are unvoiced. Movies retain original JP audio.

Japanese originals; verified English story titles for 1,513 stories and English collection titles for 103 collections. This is separate from 1,519 stories with English dialogue.

All 1,659 stories have recipes. Windows downloads and compiles original files. Browsers require user-selected original files because official source hosts block direct browser fetching. Imported chapters work offline.

Original titles are preserved. Verified alternate title languages appear where supplied. "None" under voice means no imported spoken dialogue clips; background music and effects can still be present. Movie soundtracks can contain music without spoken dialogue.

## Coverage limits

- Coverage is for pinned JP build 1.73.459696, not the latest live server.
- No relationship stories, memorial lobbies, card stories or other modes outside this catalog.
- Most chapters have no regular dialogue voices. English text uses the same original Japanese audio.
- 9 native Unity 3D cues remain explicit unavailable barriers: seven in main stories and two in event stories. Some Unity presentation and effects are approximated in the browser.
- Catalog EN titles do not imply English dialogue is available for the whole collection.
